\doxysubsection{详细描述}
\subsubsection*{template$<$\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{typename}} \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Entity}}$>$\newline
class entt\+::basic\+\_\+registry$<$ Entity $>$}
Fast and reliable entity-\/component system. 

The registry is the core class of the entity-\/component framework.~\newline
 It stores entities and arranges pools of components on a per request basis. By means of a registry, users can manage entities and components, then create views or groups to iterate them.


\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Entity} & A valid entity type (see \doxylink{structentt_1_1entt__traits}{entt\+\_\+traits} for more details). \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case of invalid entity.
\end{DoxyWarning}

Assigns the given component to an entity.   

A new instance of the given component is created and initialized with the arguments provided (the component must have a proper constructor or be of aggregate type). Then the component is assigned to the given entity.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Attempting to use an invalid entity or to assign a component to an entity that already owns it results in undefined behavior.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case of invalid entity or if the entity already owns an instance of the given component.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Type of component to create. \\
\hline
{\em Args} & Types of arguments to use to construct the component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em entity} & A valid entity identifier. \\
\hline
{\em args} & Parameters to use to initialize the component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A reference to the newly created component.   
\end{DoxyReturn}

Assigns entities to an empty registry. 

This function is intended for use in conjunction with {\ttfamily raw}.~\newline
 Don\textquotesingle{}t try to inject ranges of randomly generated entities. There is no guarantee that a registry will continue to work properly in this case.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode if all pools aren\textquotesingle{}t empty.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Assigns or replaces the given component for an entity.   

Equivalent to the following snippet (pseudocode)\+:


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\textcolor{keyword}{auto}\ \&component\ =\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a292643317d1dbb13e45824f757bd1086}{registry}}.has<\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a1dc27d36612e8fea144a96beb9cbf52b}{Component}}>(\mbox{\hyperlink{classentity}{entity}})\ ?\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a292643317d1dbb13e45824f757bd1086}{registry}}.replace<\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a1dc27d36612e8fea144a96beb9cbf52b}{Component}}>(\mbox{\hyperlink{classentity}{entity}},\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{args}}...)\ :\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a292643317d1dbb13e45824f757bd1086}{registry}}.emplace<\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a1dc27d36612e8fea144a96beb9cbf52b}{Component}}>(\mbox{\hyperlink{classentity}{entity}},\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{args}}...);}

\end{DoxyCode}


Prefer this function anyway because it has slightly better performance.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Attempting to use an invalid entity results in undefined behavior.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case of invalid entity.

Visits a registry and returns the types for its context variables. 

The signature of the function should be equivalent to the following\+:


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{void}}(\textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a4780f9d4e975066afb16b1309c644995}{id\_type}});}

\end{DoxyCode}


Returned identifiers are those of the context variables currently set.

Returns the actual version for an entity identifier. 

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Attempting to use an entity that doesn\textquotesingle{}t belong to the registry results in undefined behavior. An entity belongs to the registry even if it has been previously destroyed and/or recycled.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode if the registry doesn\textquotesingle{}t own the given entity.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em entity} & A valid entity identifier. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
Actual version for the given entity identifier. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Direct access to the list of entities of a given pool. 

The returned pointer is such that range {\ttfamily \mbox{[}data\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}Component\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}(), data\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}Component\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}() + size\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}Component\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}()\mbox{]}} is always a valid range, even if the container is empty.

There are no guarantees on the order of the entities. Use a view if you want to iterate entities and components in the expected order.


\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Type of component in which one is interested. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A pointer to the array of entities. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Iterates all the entities that are still in use. 

The function object is invoked for each entity that is still in use.~\newline
 The signature of the function should be equivalent to the following\+:


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{void}}(\textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{Entity}});}

\end{DoxyCode}


This function is fairly slow and should not be used frequently. However, it\textquotesingle{}s useful for iterating all the entities still in use, regardless of their components.

Assigns the given component to an entity. 

A new instance of the given component is created and initialized with the arguments provided (the component must have a proper constructor or be of aggregate type). Then the component is assigned to the given entity.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Attempting to use an invalid entity or to assign a component to an entity that already owns it results in undefined behavior.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case of invalid entity or if the entity already owns an instance of the given component.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Type of component to create. \\
\hline
{\em Args} & Types of arguments to use to construct the component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em entity} & A valid entity identifier. \\
\hline
{\em args} & Parameters to use to initialize the component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A reference to the newly created component. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

In case the entity doesn\textquotesingle{}t own the component, the parameters provided are used to construct it.~\newline

Returns a group for the given components. 

This kind of objects are created on the fly and share with the registry its internal data structures.~\newline
 Feel free to discard a group after the use. Creating and destroying a group is an incredibly cheap operation because they do not require any type of initialization, but for the first time they are requested.~\newline
 As a rule of thumb, storing a group should never be an option.

Groups support exclusion lists and can own types of components. The more types are owned by a group, the faster it is to iterate entities and components.~\newline
 However, groups also affect some features of the registry such as the creation and destruction of components, which will consequently be slightly slower (nothing that can be noticed in most cases).

\begin{DoxyNote}{注解}
Pools of components that are owned by a group cannot be sorted anymore. The group takes the ownership of the pools and arrange components so as to iterate them as fast as possible.
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Owned} & Types of components owned by the group. \\
\hline
{\em Get} & Types of components observed by the group. \\
\hline
{\em Exclude} & Types of components used to filter the group. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A newly created group. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Returns a temporary object to use to load snapshots. 

A snapshot is either a full or a partial dump of a registry.~\newline
 It can be used to save and restore its internal state or to keep two or more instances of this class in sync, as an example in a client-\/server architecture.

\begin{DoxyNote}{注解}
The loader returned by this function requires that the registry be empty. In case it isn\textquotesingle{}t, all the data will be automatically deleted before to return.
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A temporary object to use to load snasphosts. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Returns a sink object for the given component. 

A sink is an opaque object used to connect listeners to components.~\newline
 The sink returned by this function can be used to receive notifications whenever a new instance of the given component is created and assigned to an entity.

The function type for a listener is equivalent to\+:


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{void}}(\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{registry<Entity>}}\ \&,\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{Entity}});}

\end{DoxyCode}


Listeners are invoked {\bfseries{after}} the component has been assigned to the entity.

Iterates orphans and applies them the given function object. 

The function object is invoked for each entity that is still in use and has no components assigned.~\newline
 The signature of the function should be equivalent to the following\+:


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{void}}(\textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{Entity}});}

\end{DoxyCode}

There are no guarantees on the order of the components. Use a view if you want to iterate entities and components in the expected order.

Removes all the components from an entity and makes it orphaned. 

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
In case there are listeners that observe the destruction of components and assign other components to the entity in their bodies, the result of invoking this function may not be as expected. Attempting to use an invalid entity results in undefined behavior.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case of invalid entity.
\end{DoxyWarning}

Replaces the given component for an entity. 

A new instance of the given component is created and initialized with the arguments provided (the component must have a proper constructor or be of aggregate type). Then the component is assigned to the given entity.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Attempting to use an invalid entity or to replace a component of an entity that doesn\textquotesingle{}t own it results in undefined behavior.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case of invalid entity or if the entity doesn\textquotesingle{}t own an instance of the given component.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Type of component to replace. \\
\hline
{\em Args} & Types of arguments to use to construct the component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em entity} & A valid entity identifier. \\
\hline
{\em args} & Parameters to use to initialize the component. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A reference to the component being replaced. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Increases the capacity of the registry or of the pools for the given components. 

If no components are specified, the capacity of the registry is increased, that is the number of entities it contains. Otherwise the capacity of the pools for the given components is increased.~\newline
 In both cases, if the new capacity is greater than the current capacity, new storage is allocated, otherwise the method does nothing.


\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Types of components for which to reserve storage. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em cap} & Desired capacity. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Returns a runtime view for the given components. 

This kind of objects are created on the fly and share with the registry its internal data structures.~\newline
 Users should throw away the view after use. Fortunately, creating and destroying a runtime view is an incredibly cheap operation because they do not require any type of initialization.~\newline
 As a rule of thumb, storing a view should never be an option.

Runtime views are to be used when users want to construct a view from some external inputs and don\textquotesingle{}t know at compile-\/time what are the required components.~\newline
 This is particularly well suited to plugin systems and mods in general.


\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em It} & Type of input iterator. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em first} & An iterator to the first element of the range of components. \\
\hline
{\em last} & An iterator past the last element of the range of components. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A newly created runtime view. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Returns a temporary object to use to create snapshots. 

A snapshot is either a full or a partial dump of a registry.~\newline
 It can be used to save and restore its internal state or to keep two or more instances of this class in sync, as an example in a client-\/server architecture.

\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A temporary object to use to take snasphosts. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{void}} \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry}{entt\+::basic\+\_\+registry}}$<$ \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Entity}} $>$\+::sort (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{ }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Sorts two pools of components in the same way. 

The order of the elements in a pool is highly affected by assignments of components to entities and deletions. Components are arranged to maximize the performance during iterations and users should not make any assumption on the order.

It happens that different pools of components must be sorted the same way because of runtime and/or performance constraints. This function can be used to order a pool of components according to the order between the entities in another pool of components.

{\bfseries{How}} {\bfseries{it}} {\bfseries{works}} 

Being {\ttfamily A} and {\ttfamily B} the two sets where {\ttfamily B} is the master (the one the order of which rules) and {\ttfamily A} is the slave (the one to sort), after a call to this function an iterator for {\ttfamily A} will return the entities according to the following rules\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item All the entities in {\ttfamily A} that are also in {\ttfamily B} are returned first according to the order they have in {\ttfamily B}.
\item All the entities in {\ttfamily A} that are not in {\ttfamily B} are returned in no particular order after all the other entities.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Any subsequent change to {\ttfamily B} won\textquotesingle{}t affect the order in {\ttfamily A}.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Pools of components owned by a group cannot be sorted.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case the pool is owned by a group.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em To} & Type of components to sort. \\
\hline
{\em From} & Type of components to use to sort. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

Args$>$ \\
\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{void}} \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry}{entt\+::basic\+\_\+registry}}$<$ \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Entity}} $>$\+::sort (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Compare}}}]{compare,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Sort}}}]{algo = {\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Sort}}\{\}},  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a696e43e4d153ebfc9370d2f9b5fe3984}{Args}} \&\&...}]{args }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Sorts the pool of entities for the given component. 

The order of the elements in a pool is highly affected by assignments of components to entities and deletions. Components are arranged to maximize the performance during iterations and users should not make any assumption on the order.~\newline
 This function can be used to impose an order to the elements in the pool of the given component. The order is kept valid until a component of the given type is assigned or removed from an entity.

The comparison function object must return {\ttfamily true} if the first element is {\itshape less} than the second one, {\ttfamily false} otherwise. The signature of the comparison function should be equivalent to one of the following\+:


\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{bool}}(\textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{Entity}},\ \textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{Entity}});}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{bool}}(\textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a1dc27d36612e8fea144a96beb9cbf52b}{Component}}\ \&,\ \textcolor{keyword}{const}\ \mbox{\hyperlink{classentt_1_1basic__registry_a1dc27d36612e8fea144a96beb9cbf52b}{Component}}\ \&);}

\end{DoxyCode}


Moreover, the comparison function object shall induce a {\itshape strict weak ordering} on the values.

The sort function oject must offer a member function template {\ttfamily \doxylink{namespaceentt_a8c24ecc5ab0055f9f2a4725c95afb29e}{operator()}} that accepts three arguments\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item An iterator to the first element of the range to sort.
\item An iterator past the last element of the range to sort.
\item A comparison function to use to compare the elements.
\end{DoxyItemize}

The comparison funtion object received by the sort function object hasn\textquotesingle{}t necessarily the type of the one passed along with the other parameters to this member function.

\begin{DoxyWarning}{警告}
Pools of components owned by a group cannot be sorted.~\newline
 An assertion will abort the execution at runtime in debug mode in case the pool is owned by a group.
\end{DoxyWarning}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Type of components to sort. \\
\hline
{\em Compare} & Type of comparison function object. \\
\hline
{\em Sort} & Type of sort function object. \\
\hline
{\em Args} & Types of arguments to forward to the sort function object. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}

\begin{DoxyParams}{参数}
{\em compare} & A valid comparison function object. \\
\hline
{\em algo} & A valid sort function object. \\
\hline
{\em args} & Arguments to forward to the sort function object, if any. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Returns a view for the given components. 

This kind of objects are created on the fly and share with the registry its internal data structures.~\newline
 Feel free to discard a view after the use. Creating and destroying a view is an incredibly cheap operation because they do not require any type of initialization.~\newline
 As a rule of thumb, storing a view should never be an option.

Views do their best to iterate the smallest set of candidate entities. In particular\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Single component views are incredibly fast and iterate a packed array of entities, all of which has the given component.
\item Multi component views look at the number of entities available for each component and pick up a reference to the smallest set of candidates to test for the given components.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Views in no way affect the functionalities of the registry nor those of the underlying pools.

\begin{DoxyNote}{注解}
Multi component views are pretty fast. However their performance tend to degenerate when the number of components to iterate grows up and the most of the entities have all the given components.~\newline
 To get a performance boost, consider using a group instead.
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyTemplParams}{模板参数}
{\em Component} & Type of components used to construct the view. \\
\hline
{\em Exclude} & Types of components used to filter the view. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyTemplParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{返回}
A newly created view. 
\end{DoxyReturn}
